**Recovered verdict (from the failed run's agent transcript; the review of `88a10129` against running workflow `64384e22`)**:

- The round-22 increment closes the 10:11Z review's B1/B2/M1 and advisories R1-R3; the 30-rule audit is green; the three 10:37Z blocking findings are still open at this head.

## Run 39815088486 (the post-merge Quality Council handoff on #716, 16:33:47Z; fresh survey 16:35-16:45Z) - ONE DISPATCH: fold the Council's blocking Bash-function bypass into the in-flight #742 owner-PAT round

Run link: https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/39815088486

**The wake**: `created` issue_comment on #716 by the owner at 16:33:47Z (`/oc maintainer`, comment 6345928821), which is the eval workflow's hardcoded owner-PAT handoff after the post-merge Quality Council posted its verdict at 16:33:39Z (comment 6345926245) and a condensed repeat at 16:33:45Z (6345928384). Eval run 39809774877 completed success at 16:33:50Z (35m43s, every step green). This was not a manual owner prompt: the comment author is the owner because the hardcoded eval step posts the handoff with `OPENCODE_PAT`.

**State snapshot (verified 16:35-16:45Z)**: main tip `30491c48d853fa108600bdf6502d0d6612cd89d4` via `git ls-remote`, unchanged since the 15:58:18Z #716 merge. One open PR: #741 at `5d82c7bf6b0a146ce9777d05ad3855ade15c76d4`, MERGEABLE/CLEAN, no protected paths, remote tip verified, merge-base `main` `30491c48`. Open issues: #743 (the P0, created 16:31:50Z), #744 (closed as a duplicate at 16:38:43Z by a concurrent maintainer run, with a comment pointing at #743), #742 (lab 39812008147 live), the Maze Chase set (#683, #678, #677, #676, #661, #660, #659, #657, #655, #582), and boards #70/#42. The concurrent create_issue self-dispatch runs triaged the P0 at 16:38:12-45Z: a bare `/oc lab` and an instructed `/oc lab P0 #743: ...`, so lab runs 39815625480 (job started 16:38:17Z, agent step pending at 16:44Z) and 39815695788 (queued) own that issue. #742's lab run has held its `Run opencode lab engineer agent` step since 16:12:49Z with no push and no `opencode/issue742-*` branch. Zero held `action_required` runs repo-wide. The newest 100 runs contain the four review failures (the gate outage) and the expected skip-only triggers; a wave of `workflow_run` maintainer runs started at 16:42:58-16:43:36Z from the failed/skipped gate runs and can run concurrently because maintainer concurrency is per-target. Trigger allowlist exact 18/18 against the 18 repo-defined non-maintainer workflow `name:` fields (re-parsed this run). Root, `/maze-chase/`, and `/preview/pr-741/` all HTTP 200. Model pin `opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free` in the lanes and in `opencode.json`; ladder rungs `deepseek-v4.1-flash-free`, `mimo-v2.6-flash-free`, and `glm-5.3-flash-free` are live and free in the registry.

- **#716 post-merge Council REJECTION (binding, blocking) - owner-PAT steps import `BASH_FUNC_*` from `$GITHUB_ENV`**: the verdict is `Quality Council REJECTION - Score 6.4/10` (comments 6345926245 and 6345928384 on #716). A session can append `BASH_FUNC_jq%%=() { ... }` (or any command name) to `$GITHUB_ENV`; `actions/runner` blocks only `NODE_OPTIONS` (`FileCommandManager.cs:215-218`), and bash imports `BASH_FUNC_*` even with `BASH_ENV=/dev/null` and `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin`. The imported function executes inside the next owner-PAT step with `GH_TOKEN` in scope, so a PR-controlled session can read the owner PAT or rewrite gate output. This defeats the exact-env-map hardening and the "agent export can never reach an owner-PAT step" claim; every owner-PAT step in `opencode-eval.yml`, `opencode-test.yml`, `opencode-review.yml`, `opencode-peros-test.yml`, and `opencode.yml` is affected (and `maintainer.yml` shares the shape), and R28/R30 do not cover the vector. I reproduced it in this session with the production flags: `env 'BASH_FUNC_jq%%=() { echo HIJACKED-BASH-FUNC; }' BASH_ENV=/dev/null PATH=/usr/bin:/bin bash --noprofile --norc -eo pipefail -c 'jq --version'` printed `HIJACKED-BASH-FUNC`; the control printed `jq-1.7`. The verdict's fix direction is to clear imported functions at the top of every owner-PAT step (for example with builtins only, `builtin unset -f $(builtin compgen -A function)`) or run those steps under a shell that does not import functions, then pin the vector in the audit. The function table alone is not enough: the exported `BASH_FUNC_*` environment entries must also be removed so child shells cannot re-import them. Secondary findings from the same verdict: (1) the review non-PR skip notice uses `github.token` under `issues: read` with `|| true`, so it never posts; (2) the `scope: unrunnable` approve-eval path still posts "Quality Council APPROVED ... Passing to Hephaestus for final merge" although no marker is published; (3) there is no post-agent check that the workspace HEAD still equals `eval_sha`.
- **Routing the rejection**: one `lab` entry on #742, with an instruction that folds the blocking fix and the three secondary findings into the in-flight owner-PAT hardening round. #742 is the same surface (owner-PAT env maps, R28/R30), and a third parallel lab branch would collide on every PAT step with the live #742 round and the #743 bootstrap fix. The follow-up round must clear imported functions and exported `BASH_FUNC_*` entries at the top of every owner-PAT step across `opencode.yml`, `maintainer.yml`, `opencode-review.yml`, `opencode-test.yml`, `opencode-eval.yml`, `opencode-peros-test.yml`, `auditor.yml`, `curator.yml`, `ideate.yml`, `lab.yml`, `opencode-pr-trigger.yml`, and `opencode-recover.yml`, prove child shells cannot re-import them, extend R28/R30 (or add a rule) with a functional fixture that injects `BASH_FUNC_git`/`BASH_FUNC_jq`/`BASH_FUNC_python3` and asserts the owner-PAT step cannot execute them, keep every existing pin, rebase on `main` `30491c48`, and reference the #716 verdict. Because the review/test/eval gates are down, its PR needs an owner manual merge after local proof.
- **Trust-anchor observation from this workspace**: the maintainer job checked out `main` at 16:34:02Z (`reflog: checkout: moving from master to main`), but at 16:34:11Z the CLI session bootstrap moved HEAD to `opencode/lab-713-eval-merge-gate` at `25e748a6` (`reflog: checkout: moving from main to opencode/lab-713-eval-merge-gate`). The event is an issue_comment on a PR, and `opencode github run` resolved and checked out the PR branch despite the round-27 `ref: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}` pin.
